### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚完成**: SciERC 2×2 matrix 两模型全部完成 + DocRED LoRA 训练两模型完成 + DocRED unconstrained eval 两模型完成。

**正在跑**: DocRED **constrained eval** 是当前关键路径——两块 GPU 各跑一个：
- `w5_qwen_docred_lora_constrained_eval` — RUNNING, cuda:1, PID 321568
- `w5_llama_docred_lora_constrained_eval` — RUNNING, cuda:0, PID 317464

**Unconstrained baselines 已到手**（constrained 出来后直接算 delta）：
- Qwen DocRED FT unconstrained: DR=0.76%, R-F1=40.86%
- Llama DocRED FT unconstrained: DR=0.49%, R-F1=43.72%

**PREPARING（低优先级）**:
- w1_cf_fallback_llama_scierc / w1_cf_fallback_llama_docred — CF fallback Llama 版，D020 已定为 analytical finding，可能不需要实际跑
- w5_llama_docred_sft_data_gen — 数据已在 w5_docred_sft_data_gen 中生成过，这个可能是残留

**等待**: 2 个 cron 回调（constrained eval 完成时自动触发）

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **w5_qwen_docred_lora_constrained_eval cron 回调到达** → 读 metrics → 计算 ΔDR 和 ΔR-F1 vs unconstrained baseline (DR=0.76%, R-F1=40.86) → update_registry → 填 Qwen DocRED 2×2 格子
- **w5_llama_docred_lora_constrained_eval cron 回调到达** → 同上 vs (DR=0.49%, R-F1=43.72) → 填 Llama DocRED 2×2 格子
- **两个 DocRED constrained eval 都完成** → spawn Worker 按 `artifacts/composability_update_checklist.md` 更新论文（P0: Table 5 加 DocRED 行 + 删 Limitations "DocRED not reported"；P1: §5.5 正文加 DocRED 数字）→ 全文数字一致性审查 → 向 Director 汇报完整 4×2 composability matrix
- **worker_code_mistral_adaptation 完成** → 读产出，评估是否需要启动 Mistral 实验线（但 May 25 deadline 极近，Mistral 可能来不及）
- **论文更新完成** → May 19 内部稿交付 → May 25 EMNLP ARR 提交

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **项目核心**: OntoCoDec 用两层约束解码（grammar + semantic）编译 IE ontology schema 为 token-level constraints，消除 LLM-based IE 的 ontology violations。
- **论文 3 个 core claims**: (1) Violation landscape — 违反率由 constraint tightness 驱动而非 ontology size；(2) Prompt insufficiency — schema-informed prompting 无法消除违反；(3) Composable constraint framework — grammar 是 semantic 的前提而非 synergy。
- **当前进展**: SciERC 2×2 composability matrix 两模型已完成（Qwen DR→0.0%, Llama DR→0.09%）。DocRED 是最后一块拼图。
- **关键决策链**: D020 (CF fallback=analytical) → D023 (reframe Claim 3) → D024/D025 (composability confirmed) → D028 (Llama SciERC confirmed) → D029 (DocRED 重跑)
- **易错点**:
  - DocRED unconstrained DR 很低（Qwen 0.76%, Llama 0.49%），constrained 后 ΔDR 可能不大——这不是 negative，因为 FT 已经大幅降低 DR，constrained 是 "last mile" 消除
  - 不要把 ZS 和 FT 的 DR reduction 直接比较——FT baseline DR 本身就低，绝对下降幅度小是预期的
  - w5_llama_docred_sft_data_gen 在 PREPARING 但数据实际已生成过（w5_docred_sft_data_gen=success），可能是残留条目
  - Mistral adaptation worker 正在跑但 deadline 很近，可能是 Director 布局下一轮实验，不要因此延迟 composability matrix 论文更新
  - memory.md 有 composability update checklist 指引，论文更新时务必参照

{
  "parentUuid": "b183d6eb-a9f9-454b-aae0-25461c6566b3",

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

1. **Two constrained evals running**: Both confirmed alive by cron (PID 317464 on cuda:0, PID 321568 on cuda:1)
2. **Resource tracking discrepancy**: cuda:0 shows as idle in resource usage, but cron confirms Llama eval PID alive. This is likely because the registry entry for `w5_llama_docred_lora_constrained_eval` might not have the GPU field properly set.


3. **Mistral worker**: 13 turns, about to finish, and there's one CPU-based worker in the pool handling the adaptation task.

I need to fix the registry entry for the Llama experiment to include proper GPU field information so resource tracking aligns with what's actually running, then report to Director.

Actually, let me reconsider — the cron job confirmed it's running, so the resource tracking tool probably uses a different mechanism. I shouldn't make changes now while it's active.

Now I'm drafting the Director report following the format rules: starting with the resource status line, then current state, keeping it brief and factual. Both GPUs are occupied with constrained eval tasks, the Mistral worker is wrapping up, and I'm waiting for cron callbacks when the evaluations finish. Looking at the timestamps, the Llama eval started around 7 hours ago based on the registry creation time, so it should complete in roughly 3-4 hours, while the Qwen eval will take about 5-6 hours total. The math checks out — 14 seconds per sample across 998 samples gives roughly 3.8 hours, but both experiments started 7+ hours ago and should be finished by now. Yet cron is reporting them still running, which suggests the constrained eval is taking longer than expected, possibly due to grammar compilation overhead or some other bottleneck.

Looking at the Llama experiment more carefully, the last update was about 2.75 hours ago when it was at 11/998 samples. If it's been running at the expected pace since then, it should be around 718 samples by now, so still in progress but not drastically behind. I shouldn't SSH in myself since that's the Worker's responsibility, but I should flag this in the report and consider spawning a Worker to check the actual status if the crons don't update soon. sense.

For Qwen, the timeline is confusing — it was created at 20:03 with an ETA of 5-6 hours, but the note shows it was only at 2/998 samples at 01:56, which seems too slow for that window. The grammar compilation for 96 relation types probably took a significant chunk of time before inference even started. Looking at the unconstrained eval which took 5.82 hours for 998 samples, the constrained version would be slower, maybe 7-8 hours total. If inference kicked off around 22:00 after compilation, I'm trying to figure out where it stands now.

Qwen should wrap up around 06:00 UTC, while Llama's ETA of 3.8 hours from 00:31 puts it finishing around 04:19 UTC — so that one should be done soon. I should report these timelines to the Director. I'm also noticing the GPU tracking is incomplete: the registry shows Qwen running on cuda:1, but the Llama experiment isn't showing up in the GPU data even though cron confirms it's active. The Llama spec probably didn't get the GPU info recorded properly.

For now, I'll focus on getting the Director update out with the current status — waiting on those cron callbacks, the Mistral worker wrapping up, and then I can push the composability matrix into the paper. I should also review the composability_update_checklist.md so I'm ready to move quickly once the evals finish.
